&lt;project_context>
# 项目: schema_semantic_constrained_ie

## 运行环境
- 服务器名: westd-16980
- SSH 连接命令: ssh connect.westd.seetacloud.com  # 必须用此命令，~/.ssh/config 自动应用密钥和跳板机，禁止用裸 IP
- 项目代码根目录: /root/schema_semantic_constrained_ie


## 环境搭建
连接服务器后先调 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果匹配，跳过基础环境搭建，只装项目特有依赖。已预装的包不要重复安装。

## 输出风格
回复简洁直接。只输出执行结果和必要信息，不要解释过程、不要客套、不要给未被要求的建议。
&lt;/project_context>
## 任务: Beam search / sampling 技术可行性检查（READ-ONLY）

### 背景
审稿 minor 指出 greedy decoding 限制。需要检查现有代码是否支持 beam search / sampling 下的 constrained decoding。

### 检查项（全部用 ssh_read_only，不改代码）

**1. 主推理脚本参数支持**
- 读取 `/root/schema_semantic_constrained_ie/run_constrained_extraction.py`（或其他主推理脚本）
- 检查是否已有 beam_size / num_beams / top_p / temperature / do_sample 参数
- 检查 `generate()` 调用的参数

**2. XGrammar constraint state 与 multi-sequence 兼容性**
- 找到 XGrammar 相关代码（可能在 `constrained_generation/` 或类似目录）
- 检查 constraint state 是否是 per-sequence 的（beam search 需要每个 beam 独立维护 state）
- 看是否有 `GrammarLogitsProcessor` 或类似类
- XGrammar 的 logits processor 通常支持 batch（每个 sequence 独立 state），确认是否如此

**3. Transformers generate() 的 logits_processor 机制**
- 检查代码中 `model.generate()` 是否传入了 `logits_processor=[...]`
- 如果是 logits_processor 方式，beam search 下 transformers 会对每个 beam 独立调用 processor
- 但需确认 semantic constraint 的 stateful processor（entity registry）是否正确处理 multi-beam

**4. Semantic layer 的 stateful constraint**
- Semantic layer（TypeConstraintProcessor）维护 entity registry（已生成的 entity 及其 type）
- Beam search 下每个 beam 可能有不同的 entity registry 状态
- 检查 TypeConstraintProcessor 是否有 clone/fork state 的能力
- 这可能是 beam search 的 blocker

### 服务器
- westd-16980, 工作目录 `/root/schema_semantic_constrained_ie/`
- `find /root/schema_semantic_constrained_ie/ -name "*.py" | head -30` 了解代码结构
- 重点文件：`run_constrained_extraction.py`, 任何 `*constraint*` 或 `*grammar*` 或 `*processor*` 相关文件

### 产出
写报告到：`/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_6/projects/schema_semantic_constrained_ie/artifacts/beam_sampling_feasibility.md`

格式：
- 可行性判断：YES / NO / NEEDS_CODE_CHANGE
- 如果 YES：列出命令行参数变更
- 如果 NEEDS_CODE_CHANGE：具体改什么、改动量
- 如果 NO：阻塞原因
- 附代码证据（文件名+行号+关键代码片段）

### ⚠️ 重要
- **只读检查，不改代码**
- 不使用 ssh_execute，全部 ssh_read_only

```text
{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Phase 2: Constrained extraction with grammar (XGrammar) + semantic (TypeConstraintProcessor) layers.\\n\\nModes:\\n  unconstrained  - no constraints (baseline)\\n  grammar_only   - XGrammar JSON/enum structure enforcement\\n  semantic_only  - TypeConstraintProcessor domain/range masking\\n  full_s1        - both layers, single-pass\\n  full_s2        - two-pass: entities first, then constrained relations\\n\\nDatasets: scierc (sentence-level), docred (document-level)\\nOutput: JSONL compatible with violation_checker.py\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nimport re\\nimport time\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport torch\\nfrom transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\\n\\nfrom compiler.ontology_compiler import ConstraintSpec, RelationConstraint\\nfrom compiler.type_mask_processor import TypeConstraintProcessor\\n\\n\\ndef _chat_template_kwargs(tokenizer):\\n    name = getattr(tokenizer, 'name_or_path', '').lower()\\n    if 'qwen' in name:\\n        return {'enable_thinking': False}\\n    return {}\\n\\n\\nRAW_TO_CANONICAL = {\\n    \\\"USED-FOR\\\": \\\"Used-for\\\", \\\"FEATURE-OF\\\": \\\"Feature-of\\\",\\n    \\\"HYPONYM-OF\\\": \\\"Hyponym-of\\\", \\\"PART-OF\\\": \\\"Part-of\\\",\\n    \\\"COMPARE\\\": \\\"Compare\\\", \\\"CONJUNCTION\\\": \\\"Conjunction\\\",\\n    \\\"EVALUATE-FOR\\\": \\\"Evaluate-for\\\",\\n}\\n\\n# DocRED entity type order must match Phase 0 (run_extraction_docred.py)\\nDOCRED_ENTITY_TYPES = [\\\"PER\\\", \\\"ORG\\\", \\\"LOC\\\", \\\"TIME\\\", \\\"NUM\\\", \\\"MISC\\\"]\\n\\nSCIERC_SYSTEM = \\\"\\\"\\\"You are an expert information extraction system for scientific texts. offset:h_e - offset + 1])\\n                        tail_text = \\\" \\\".join(tokens[t_s - offset:t_e - offset + 1])\\n                        head_type = span_type.get((h_s, h_e), \\\"UNKNOWN\\\")\\n                        tail_type = span_type.get((t_s, t_e), \\\"UNKNOWN\\\")\\n                        rtype = RAW_TO_CANONICAL.get(rtype_raw, rtype_raw)\\n                        gold_relations.append({\\n                            \\\"head\\\": head_text, \\\"tail\\\": tail_text, \\\"type\\\": rtype,\\n                            \\\"head_type\\\": head_type, \\\"tail_type\\\": tail_type,\\n \\n\\n... compiled_grammar = None\\n    entity_compiled_grammar = None\\n    xgr_compiler = None\\n\\n    needs_grammar = args.mode in (\\\"grammar_only\\\", \\\"full_s1\\\", \\\"full_s2\\\")\\n    if entity_compiled_grammar = xgr_compiler.compile_grammar(grammar)\\n            print(\\\"Grammar compiled (entity-only for S2 Pass 1)\\\")\\n\\n    Path(args.output_path).parent.mkdir(parents=True, s[\\\"doc_id\\\"] not in processed_ids]\\n    skipped = len(samples) - len(todo)\\n    if args.resume and skipped > 0:\\n        print(f\\\"Resuming from {skipped} processed samples, {len(todo)} remaining\\\")\\n\\n    file_mode = \\\"a\\\" if args.resume else \\\"w\\\"\\n    n_ok, n_fail = 0, 0\\n\\n    with open(args.output_path, file_mode) as fout:\\n        for idx, (i, sample) in enumerate(todo):\\n            t0 = time.time()\\n\\n            if args.mode == \\\"full_s2\\\":\\n                raw, parsed = run_s2_two_pass(\\n                    model, tokenizer, sample, args.dataset, spec,\\n                    entity_compiled_grammar, xgr_compiler, args.max_new_tokens,\\n                )\\n            else:\\n                raw, parsed = run_single_pass(\\n                    model, tokenizer, sample, args.dataset, spec,\\n                    args.mode, compiled_grammar, args.max_new_tokens,\\n                )\\n\\n            elapsed = time.time() - t0\\n\\n Inside relation's \\\"tail_type\\\" field value\\n    FREE_TEXT = auto()           # Entity text, relation head/tail text, etc.\\n\\n\\n@dataclass\\nclass EntityRegistry:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Runtime registry of generated entities and their types.\\n\\n    Tracks text->type mappings from the entities list so that when a relation\\n    references an entity by text, we know its type for domain/range checking.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    entities: dict[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict)\\n\\n    def register(self, text: str, entity_type: str):\\n        self.entities[text] = entity_type\\n\\n    def get_type(self, text: str) -> Optional[str]:\\n        return self.entities.get(text)\\n\\n    def clear(self):\\n        self.entities.clear()\\n\\n\\n@dataclass\\nclass GenerationState:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Tracks the state of JSON generation for constraint application.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    position: FieldPosition = FieldPosition.UNKNOWN\\n    current_relation_type: Optional[str] = None\\n    current_head_type: Optional[str] = None\\n    current_head_text: Optional[str] = None\\n    current_tail_text: Optional[str] = None\\n    entity_registry: EntityRegistry = field(default_factory=EntityRegistry)\\n    in_entities_section: bool = True\\n\\n    def reset(self):\\n        self.position = FieldPosition.UNKNOWN\\n        self.current_relation_type = None\\n        self.current_head_type = None\\n        self.current_head_text = None\\n        self.current_tail_text = None\\n        self.entity_registry.clear()\\n        self.in_entities_section = True\\n\\n\\nclass PositionDetector:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Detects current field position by parsing generated text.\\n\\n    Regex-based parsing of JSON text generated so far. If entity type known AND D/R-valid → force that single type\\n        - If entity type known but NOT D/R-valid → use D/R constraint\\n        - If entity type unknown → use D/R constraint\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        rel_type = self.state.current_relation_type\\n        head_type = self.state.current_head_type\\n        constraint = self.spec.get_constraint(rel_type) if rel_type else None\\n\\n        if constraint is not None:\\n            if head_type is not None:\\n                allowed_dr = constraint.valid_tail_types_for_head(head_type)\\n            else:\\n                allowed_dr = constraint.valid_tail_types()\\n        else:\\n            allowed_dr = None\\n\\n        tail_text = self.state.current_tail_text\\n        if tail_text:\\n            actual_type = self.state.entity_registry.get_type(tail_text)\\n            if actual_type and actual_type in self._type_first_token:\\n                if allowed_dr is None or actual_type in allowed_dr:\\n                    return self._apply_type_mask(logits, {actual_type})\\n\\n        if allowed_dr is not None:\\n            return self._apply_type_mask(logits, allowed_dr)\\n        return logits\\n\\n    def _apply_type_mask(\\n        self,\\n        logits: torch.FloatTensor,\\n        allowed_types: set[str],\\n    ) -> torch.FloatTensor:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Set logits of disallowed type first-tokens to -inf.\\n\\n        Only masks the FIRST token of each type string. generate() calls.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        self.state.reset()\\n\\n\\n# ── Integration Helper ───────────────────────────────────────────────────\\n\\ndef build_constrained_processors(\\n as xgr\\n    from .ontology_compiler import compile_ontology\\n\\n    spec, compiled_grammar = compile_ontology(constraints_path, tokenizer)\\n\\n    processors = []\\n\\n    if compiled_grammar is not None:\\n        grammar_processor = xgr.contrib.hf.LogitsProcessor(compiled_grammar)\\n        processors.append(grammar_processor)\\n\\n    type_processor = TypeConstraintProcessor(spec, {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Phase 2: Constrained extraction with grammar (XGrammar) + semantic (TypeConstraintProcessor) layers.\\n\\nModes:\\n  unconstrained  - no constraints (baseline)\\n  grammar_only   - XGrammar JSON/enum structure enforcement\\n  semantic_only  - TypeConstraintProcessor domain/range masking\\n  full_s1        - both layers, single-pass\\n  full_s2        - two-pass: entities first, then constrained relations\\n\\nDatasets: scierc (sentence-level), docred (document-level)\\nOutput: JSONL compatible with violation_checker.py\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nimport re\\nimport time\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport torch\\nfrom transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\\n\\nfrom compiler.ontology_compiler import ConstraintSpec, RelationConstraint\\nfrom compiler.type_mask_processor import TypeConstraintProcessor\\n\\n\\ndef _chat_template_kwargs(tokenizer):\\n    name = getattr(tokenizer, 'name_or_path', '').lower()\\n    if 'qwen' in name:\\n        return {'enable_thinking': False}\\n    return {}\\n\\n\\nRAW_TO_CANONICAL = {\\n    \\\"USED-FOR\\\": \\\"Used-for\\\", \\\"FEATURE-OF\\\": \\\"Feature-of\\\",\\n    \\\"HYPONYM-OF\\\": \\\"Hyponym-of\\\", \\\"PART-OF\\\": \\\"Part-of\\\",\\n    \\\"COMPARE\\\": \\\"Compare\\\", \\\"CONJUNCTION\\\": \\\"Conjunction\\\",\\n    \\\"EVALUATE-FOR\\\": \\\"Evaluate-for\\\",\\n}\\n\\n# DocRED entity type order must match Phase 0 (run_extraction_docred.py)\\nDOCRED_ENTITY_TYPES = [\\\"PER\\\", \\\"ORG\\\", \\\"LOC\\\", \\\"TIME\\\", \\\"NUM\\\", \\\"MISC\\\"]\\n\\nSCIERC_SYSTEM = \\\"\\\"\\\"You are an expert information extraction system for scientific texts.      \\\"\\\"\\\"Apply domain/range constraints to logits.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            input_ids: [batch_size, seq_len] -- full sequence including prompt\\n            scores: [batch_size, vocab_size] -- logits for next token\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            Modified scores with disallowed type tokens set to -inf\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        batch_size = input_ids.shape[0]\\n\\n        for batch_idx in range(batch_size):\\n            generated_ids = input_ids[batch_idx, self.prompt_length:].tolist()\\n            generated_text = self.tokenizer.decode(\\n                generated_ids, skip_special_tokens=True\\n            )\\n\\n            position = self._detect_position(generated_text)\\n\\n            if position == FieldPosition.ENTITY_TYPE:\\n                scores[batch_idx] = self._mask_entity_type(scores[batch_idx])\\n\\n            elif position == FieldPosition.RELATION_HEAD_TYPE:\\n                scores[batch_idx] = self._mask_head_type(scores[batch_idx])\\n\\n            elif position == FieldPosition.RELATION_TAIL_TYPE:\\n                scores[batch_idx] = self._mask_tail_type(scores[batch_idx])\\n\\n        return scores\\n\\n    def _mask_entity_type(self, logits: torch.FloatTensor) -> torch.FloatTensor:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"At entity type position, allow only valid entity type first tokens.\\n\\n        XGrammar's enum constraint already restricts to valid types, but this\\n        provides defense-in-depth by masking at the logit level.\\n        All entity types are allowed here (no domain/range filtering at this stage).\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        return self._apply_type_mask(logits, set(self.spec.entity_types))\\n\\n    def _mask_head_type(self, logits: torch.FloatTensor) -> torch.FloatTensor:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Mask entity types that cannot be head_type for the current relation.\\n\\n        Strategy: intersect entity registry type with D/R-valid types.\\n        - If entity type known AND D/R-valid → force that single type\\n        - If entity type known but NOT D/R-valid → use D/R constraint\\n        - If entity type unknown → use D/R constraint\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        rel_type = self.state.current_relation_type\\n        constraint = self.spec.get_constraint(rel_type) if rel_type else None\\n        allowed_dr = constraint.valid_head_types() if constraint else None\\n\\n        head_text = self.state.current_head_text\\n        if head_text:\\n            actual_type = self.state.entity_registry.get_type(head_text)\\n            if actual_type and actual_type in self._type_first_token:\\n                if allowed_dr is None or actual_type in allowed_dr:\\n                    return self._apply_type_mask(logits, {actual_type})\\n\\n        if allowed_dr is not None:\\n            return self._apply_type_mask(logits, allowed_dr)\\n        return logits\\n\\n    def _mask_tail_type(self, logits: torch.FloatTensor) -> torch.FloatTensor:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Mask entity types that cannot be tail_type for (relation, head_type).\\n\\n        Strategy: intersect entity registry type with D/R-valid types.\\n        - If entity type known AND D/R-valid → force that single type\\n        - If entity type known but NOT D/R-valid → use D/R constraint\\n        - If entity type unknown → use D/R constraint\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        rel_type = self.state.current_relation_type\\n        head_type = self.state.current_head_type\\n        constraint = self.spec.get_constraint(rel_type) if rel_type else None\\n\\n        if constraint is not None:\\n            if head_type is not None:\\n                allowed_dr = constraint.valid_tail_types_for_head(head_type)\\n            else:\\n                allowed_dr = constraint.valid_tail_types()\\n        else:\\n            allowed_dr = None\\n\\n        tail_text self._type_first_token:\\n                if allowed_dr is None or actual_type in allowed_dr:\\n                    return self._apply_type_mask(logits, {actual_type})\\n\\n        if allowed_dr is Called at every\\n    decoding step, so only inspects the tail of the text.\\n\\n    Future optimization: use XGrammar's GrammarMatcher state for O(1) per step\\n    instead of O(n) text parsing.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    _RE_TAIL_TYPE = re.compile(r'\\\"tail_type\\\"\\\\s*:\\\\s*\\\"[^\\\"]*$')\\n    _RE_HEAD_TYPE = re.compile(r'\\\"head_type\\\"\\\\s*:\\\\s*\\\"[^\\\"]*$')\\n    _RE_TYPE_FIELD = re.compile(r'\\\"type\\\"\\\\s*:\\\\s*\\\"[^\\\"]*$')\\n\\n    _RE_ENTITY = re.compile(r'\\\"text\\\"\\\\s*:\\\\s*\\\"([^\\\"]+)\\\"\\\\s*,\\\\s*\\\"type\\\"\\\\s*:\\\\s*\\\"([^\\\"]+)\\\"')\\n\\n    _RE_REL_TYPE_VAL = re.compile(r'\\\"type\\\"\\\\s*:\\\\s*\\\"([^\\\"]+)\\\"')\\n    _RE_HEAD_TYPE_VAL = re.compile(r'\\\"head_type\\\"\\\\s*:\\\\s*\\\"([^\\\"]+)\\\"')\\n    _RE_HEAD_TEXT_VAL = re.compile(r'\\\"head\\\"\\\\s*:\\\\s*\\\"([^\\\"]+)\\\"')\\n    _RE_TAIL_TEXT_VAL = re.compile(r'\\\"tail\\\"\\\\s*:\\\\s*\\\"([^\\\"]+)\\\"')\\n    _RE_REL_START = re.compile(r'\\\\{\\\\s*\\\"head\\\"')\\n\\n    @classmethod\\n    def detect(cls, generated_text: str, state: GenerationState) -> FieldPosition:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Determine which JSON field is currently being generated.\\n\\n        Mutates state in place: updates entity registry, current relation type,\\n        current head type, etc.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        if '\\\"relations\\\"' in generated_text:\\n            state.in_entities_section = False\\n\\n        for match in cls._RE_ENTITY.finditer(generated_text):\\n            state.entity_registry.register(match.group(1), match.group(2))\\n\\n        # Find the last relation object being generated\\n        last_rel_start = -1\\n        for m in cls._RE_REL_START.finditer(generated_text):\\n            last_rel_start = m.start()\\n\\n        if last_rel_start >= 0:\\n            partial_rel = generated_text[last_rel_start:]\\n\\n            m = cls._RE_REL_TYPE_VAL.search(partial_rel)\\n            if m:\\n                state.current_relation_type = m.group(1)\\n\\n            m = cls._RE_HEAD_TYPE_VAL.search(partial_rel)\\n            if m:\\n                state.current_head_type = m.group(1)\\n\\n            m = cls._RE_HEAD_TEXT_VAL.search(partial_rel)\\n            if m:\\n                state.current_head_text = m.group(1)\\n\\n            m = cls._RE_TAIL_TEXT_VAL.search(partial_rel)\\n            if m:\\n                state.current_tail_text = m.group(1)\\n\\n        # Determine position from last 100 chars\\n        tail = generated_text[-100:]\\n\\n        if cls._RE_TAIL_TYPE.search(tail):\\n            return FieldPosition.RELATION_TAIL_TYPE\\n\\n        if cls._RE_HEAD_TYPE.search(tail):\\n            return FieldPosition.RELATION_HEAD_TYPE\\n\\n        if cls._RE_TYPE_FIELD.search(tail):\\n            if state.in_entities_section:\\n                return FieldPosition.ENTITY_TYPE\\n            else:\\n                return FieldPosition.RELATION_TYPE\\n\\n        return FieldPosition.FREE_TEXT\\n\\n\\nclass TypeConstraintProcessor(LogitsProcessor):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Enforces domain/range type constraints during autoregressive generation.\\n\\n    Constraint logic:\\n    - ENTITY_TYPE: Allow all entity type first tokens (defense-in-depth with XGrammar)\\n    - RELATION_HEAD_TYPE: Given relation R, allow types T where exists T' s.t. {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nThis file helps integrate xgrammar in HF transformers package by extending\\ntransformers.LogitsProcessor, which is to be fed to `model.generate()`.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nfrom typing import List, Union\\n\\nimport torch\\nimport transformers\\n\\nimport xgrammar as xgr\\n\\n\\nclass LogitsProcessor(transformers.LogitsProcessor):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    LogitsProcessor for processing logits in transformers' generate() method.\\n\\n    Example usage\\n    -------------\\n        .. code:: python\\n\\n            model_name = \\\"Qwen/Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct\\\"\\n            tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(model_name)\\n            config = AutoConfig.from_pretrained(model_name)\\n            # This can be larger than tokenizer.vocab_size due to paddings\\n            full_vocab_size = config.vocab_size\\n            tokenizer_info = xgr.TokenizerInfo.from_huggingface(tokenizer, vocab_size=full_vocab_size)\\n\\n            grammar_compiler = xgr.GrammarCompiler(tokenizer_info)\\n            compiled_grammar = grammar_compiler.compile_builtin_json_grammar()\\n            xgr_logits_processor = xgr.contrib.hf.LogitsProcessor(compiled_grammar)\\n            model.generate(prompt, logits_processor=[xgr_logits_processor])\\n\\n        For an end-to-end example, see folder `examples/hf_transformers/`.\\n\\n    Notes\\n    -----\\n        - Note that this LogitsProcessor can only be used once. For each `generate()` call,\\n            instantiate a new one.\\n        - Note that this implementation may contain extra overhead.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, compiled_grammar: Union[xgr.CompiledGrammar, List[xgr.CompiledGrammar]]):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Initialize the LogitsProcessor.\\n\\n        Parameters\\n        ----------\\n        compiled_grammar : xgr.CompiledGrammar | List[xgr.CompiledGrammar]\\n            One or more grammars compiled according to the given grammar and the model's tokenizer_info.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        self.matchers: List[xgr.GrammarMatcher] = []\\n        self.compiled_grammars: List[xgr.CompiledGrammar] = (\\n            compiled_grammar if isinstance(compiled_grammar, list) else [compiled_grammar]\\n        )\\n        self.full_vocab_size = self.compiled_grammars[0].tokenizer_info.vocab_size\\n        self.token_bitmask = None\\n        self.prefilled = False\\n        self.batch_size = 0\\n\\n    def __call__(self, input_ids: torch.LongTensor, scores: torch.FloatTensor) -> torch.FloatTensor:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Accept token sampled in the last iteration, fill in bitmask, and apply bitmask to logits.\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            scores: Logits modified with bitmask.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        # Lazily initialize GrammarMatchers and bitmask\\n        if len(self.matchers) == 0:\\n            self.batch_size = input_ids.shape[0]\\n            self.compiled_grammars = (\\n                self.compiled_grammars\\n                if len(self.compiled_grammars) > 1\\n                else self.compiled_grammars * self.batch_size\\n            )\\n            assert (\\n                len(self.compiled_grammars) == self.batch_size\\n            ), \\\"The number of compiled grammars must be equal to the batch size.\\\"\\n            self.matchers = [\\n                xgr.GrammarMatcher(self.compiled_grammars[i]) for i in range(self.batch_size)\\n            ]\\n            self.token_bitmask = xgr.allocate_token_bitmask(self.batch_size, self.full_vocab_size)\\n\\n        if input_ids.shape[0] != self.batch_size:\\n            raise RuntimeError(\\n                \\\"Expect input_ids.shape[0] to be LogitsProcessor.batch_size.\\\"\\n                + f\\\"Got {input_ids.shape[0]} for the former, and {self.batch_size} for the latter.\\\"\\n            )\\n\\n        if not self.prefilled:\\n            # Have not sampled a token yet\\n            self.prefilled = True\\n        else:\\n            for i in range(self.batch_size):\\n                if not self.matchers[i].is_terminated():\\n                    sampled_token = input_ids[i][-1]\\n                    assert self.matchers[i].accept_token(sampled_token)\\n\\n        for i in range(self.batch_size):\\n            if not self.matchers[i].is_terminated():\\n                self.matchers[i].fill_next_token_bitmask(self.token_bitmask, i)\\n\\n        # We only support masking logits on CUDA or CPU\\n        device_type = scores.device.type\\n        if device_type != \\\"cuda\\\":\\n            scores = scores.to(\\\"cpu\\\")\\n        xgr.apply_token_bitmask_inplace(scores, self.token_bitmask.to(scores.device))\\n        if device_type != \\\"cuda\\\":\\n            scores = scores.to(device_type)\\n\\n        # NOTE: Cannot reset here because __call__ is not invoked when stop token\\n        # is sampled. This is why each `generate()` call needs to instantiate an\\n        # LogitsProcessor\\n\\n        return scores\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.43}}"}
```

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I have all the information I need. Let me analyze the findings:

1. **Main script parameters**: No beam_size/num_beams/top_p/temperature/do_sample parameters in argparse. All `generate()` calls use `do_sample=False` (greedy).

2. **XGrammar HF LogitsProcessor**:
   - On first call, it lazily initializes `self.batch_size = input_ids.shape[0]` matchers - one per item in the batch.
   - For beam search, HF transformers expands the batch by `num_beams` (e.g., batch_size=1 with num_beams=5 → input_ids.shape[0]=5 on first call).
   - Each beam gets its own `GrammarMatcher` with independent grammar state, which works correctly with beam search. The LogitsProcessor is instantiated fresh for each `generate()` call as required.

3. **TypeConstraintProcessor (semantic layer)**:
   - Has a SINGLE `self.state = GenerationState()` instance.
   - The `__call__` method processes all batch items using this shared state, and `_detect_position` mutates it based on the generated text. With beam search, where `input_ids.shape[0] = num_beams`, each beam has different text but they're all corrupting the same state object. When beam 0 finishes processing, its mutations to `entity_registry` and `current_relation_type` are already baked in before beam 1 even starts, making the state unreliable for subsequent beams. This is the fundamental blocker preventing beam search from working.

The specific failure modes are clear: entities registered by one beam persist in the registry even if another beam never generates them, and state variables like `current_relation_type` get overwritten repeatedly as each beam's parsing updates them. However, sampling with a single sequence would actually work fine since there's only one state to maintain—I'd just need to add the sampling parameters without any code restructuring.

To fix beam search properly, I need to refactor TypeConstraintProcessor to maintain a separate GenerationState object for each beam, then thread that state through the `__call__` method, `_detect_position`, and the masking functions. This is roughly 30-50 lines of changes.
```

  XGrammar's enum constraint already restricts to valid types, but this\\n        provides defense-in-depth by masking at the logit level.\\n        All entity types are allowed here (no domain/range filtering at this stage).\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        return self._apply_type_mask(logits, set(self.spec.entity_types))\\n\\n    def _mask_head_type(self, logits: torch.FloatTensor) -> torch.FloatTensor:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Mask entity types that cannot be head_type for the current relation.\\n\\n        Strategy: intersect entity registry type with D/R-valid types.\\n        - If entity type known AND D/R-valid → force that single type\\n        - If entity type known but NOT D/R-valid → use D/R constraint\\n        - If entity type unknown → use D/R constraint\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        rel_type = self.state.current_relation_type\\n        constraint = self.spec.get_constraint(rel_type) if rel_type else None\\n        allowed_dr = constraint.valid_head_types() {actual_type})\\n\\n        if allowed_dr is not None:\\n            return self._apply_type_mask(logits, allowed_dr)\\n        return logits\\n\\n    def _mask_tail_type(self, logits: torch.FloatTensor) -> torch.FloatTensor:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Mask entity types that cannot be tail_type for (relation, head_type).\\n\\n        Strategy: intersect entity registry type with D/R-valid types.\\n        - If entity type known AND D/R-valid → force that single type\\n        - If entity type known but NOT D/R-valid → use D/R constraint\\n        - If entity type unknown → use D/R constraint\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        rel_type = self.state.current_relation_type\\n        head_type = self.state.current_head_type\\n        constraint = {
````json
  "file_path": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_6/projects/schema_semantic_constrained_ie/artifacts/beam_sampling_feasibility.md",
  "content": "# Beam Search / Sampling 可行性检查报告\n\n## 结论\n\n| 方法 | 可行性 | 说明 |\n|------|--------|------|\n| **Sampling** (temperature/top_p/top_k) | **YES** | batch_size=1 下无需改代码，只需加 CLI 参数 |\n| **Beam Search** (num_beams > 1) | **NEEDS_CODE_CHANGE** | TypeConstraintProcessor 单一共享 state 是 blocker |\n\n---\n\n## 1. 当前代码现状\n\n### 参数支持\n\n`run_constrained_extraction.py` (L602-615) argparse 中**无任何** beam/sampling 参数：\n\n```python\nparser.add_argument(\"--max_new_tokens\", type=int, default=2048)\n# 无 num_beams, do_sample, temperature, top_p, top_k\n```\n\n所有 `generate()` 调用（L441, L451, L483, L494, L542, L553）均硬编码 `do_sample=False`（greedy）。\n\n### generate() 调用方式\n\n`run_constrained_extraction.py:436-441`:\n```python\ngen_kwargs = dict(**inputs, max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens, do_sample=False)\nif processors:\n    gen_kwargs[\"logits_processor\"] = processors\nwith torch.no_grad():\n    output_ids = model.generate(**gen_kwargs)\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2. XGrammar Layer — 多 beam 兼容性 ✅\n\n`/root/miniconda3/lib/python3.12/site-packages/xgrammar/contrib/hf.py`:\n\n```python\ndef __call__(self, input_ids, scores):\n    if len(self.matchers) == 0:\n        self.batch_size = input_ids.shape[0]  # 自动适配 beam 数\n        self.matchers = [\n            xgr.GrammarMatcher(self.compiled_grammars[i]) for i in range(self.batch_size)\n        ]\n```\n\nXGrammar 在首次调用时根据 `input_ids.shape[0]`（beam search 下 = num_beams）创建**独立的 GrammarMatcher per beam**。每个 matcher 独立追踪 grammar state。**完全兼容 beam search。**\n\n---\n\n## 3. TypeConstraintProcessor (Semantic Layer) — Beam Search Blocker ❌\n\n`compiler/type_mask_processor.py:188-200`:\n```python\nclass TypeConstraintProcessor(LogitsProcessor):\n    def __init__(self, spec, tokenizer, prompt_length=0):\n        self.state = GenerationState()  # ← 单一 state 实例\n```\n\n`__call__` (L238-272):\n```python\ndef __call__(self, input_ids, scores):\n    batch_size = input_ids.shape[0]\n    for batch_idx in range(batch_size):\n        generated_text = self.tokenizer.decode(input_ids[batch_idx, self.prompt_length:].tolist(), ...)\n        position = self._detect_position(generated_text)  # ← 修改 self.state\n        if position == FieldPosition.RELATION_HEAD_TYPE:\n            scores[batch_idx] = self._mask_head_type(scores[batch_idx])  # ← 读 self.state\n```\n\n**问题**：`_detect_position()` 调用 `PositionDetector.detect(generated_text, self.state)` 会**修改** `self.state`（entity_registry、current_relation_type、current_head_type 等）。Beam search 下 batch_size = num_beams，循环中每个 beam 的 parsing 都会**覆盖**同一个 state。\n\n具体影响：\n- Beam 0 注册 entity \"BERT\" → type \"Method\"，写入 `self.state.entity_registry`\n- Beam 1 解析时可能尚未生成 \"BERT\"，但 registry 已污染\n- `_mask_head_type()` / `_mask_tail_type()` 读取 `self.state.current_relation_type` 时得到的是最后一个 beam 的值\n\n---\n\n## 4. Sampling 的可行性\n\nSampling（`do_sample=True, temperature=T, top_p=P`）在 **batch_size=1** 下执行，`input_ids.shape[0]` 始终为 1，不存在 state 冲突。\n\n**所需改动（纯参数传递，不涉及 processor 逻辑）：**\n\n```python\n# argparse 加 3 个参数\nparser.add_argument(\"--do_sample\", action=\"store_true\")\nparser.add_argument(\"--temperature\", type=float, default=1.0)\nparser.add_argument(\"--top_p\", type=float, default=1.0)\n\n# generate() 调用改为\ngen_kwargs = dict(**inputs, max_new_tokens=max_new_tokens,\n                  do_sample=args.do_sample,\n                  temperature=args.temperature,\n                  top_p=args.top_p)\n```\n\n改动量：~10 行。\n\n---\n\n## 5. Beam Search 所需改动\n\n### 核心改动：per-beam state\n\n`TypeConstraintProcessor` 需要维护 `List[GenerationState]` 而非单一 `GenerationState`：\n\n```python\nclass TypeConstraintProcessor(LogitsProcessor):\n    def __init__(self, spec, tokenizer, prompt_length=0, num_beams=1):\n        self.states = [GenerationState() for _ in range(num_beams)]\n\n    def __call__(self, input_ids, scores):\n        if len(self.states) < input_ids.shape[0]:\n            # 延迟初始化到实际 beam 数\n            self.states = [GenerationState() for _ in range(input_ids.shape[0])]\n\n        for batch_idx in range(input_ids.shape[0]):\n            generated_text = self.tokenizer.decode(...)\n            position = self.detector.detect(generated_text, self.states[batch_idx])\n            # masking 函数也需接受 state 参数\n            if position == FieldPosition.RELATION_HEAD_TYPE:\n                scores[batch_idx] = self._mask_head_type(scores[batch_idx], self.states[batch_idx])\n```\n\n### 改动清单\n\n| 文件 | 改动 |\n|------|------|\n| `compiler/type_mask_processor.py` | `self.state` → `self.states: List[GenerationState]`；`_mask_head_type`/`_mask_tail_type` 加 state 参数 |\n| `compiler/type_mask_processor_cf_fallback.py` | 同上（相同结构） |\n| `run_constrained_extraction.py` | 加 `--num_beams` 参数，传入 generate() |\n\n### 改动量估算\n\n- `type_mask_processor.py`: ~30 行改动（state 初始化 + 函数签名 + 索引）\n- `type_mask_processor_cf_fallback.py`: ~30 行（相同改动）\n- `run_constrained_extraction.py`: ~15 行（argparse + gen_kwargs）\n- **总计：~75 行**\n\n### 额外注意\n\n- HF `generate()` 在 beam search 中可能做 beam reordering（选出 top-k beams 后重排序）。此时 `input_ids` 的顺序会变化，`states` 列表需要对应重排。不过由于 `PositionDetector.detect()` 每步都从完整 generated_text 重新解析（无增量 state），所以 reordering 不会导致 state 不一致——每步都是从文本重建 state。**这意味着 per-beam state 实际上不需要跨步维护**，因为 detect() 每次 full re-parse。\n- 但 `entity_registry` 是累积的——每步 regex finditer 全文，所以即使 beam reorder 也能正确重建。\n\n**结论：改动后 beam search 直接可行，无需处理 beam reorder。**\n\n---\n\n## 6. 总结\n\n| 场景 | 可行性 | 改动量 | 风险 |\n|------|--------|--------|------|\n| Sampling (temp/top_p) | ✅ 立即可用 | ~10 行 | 无 |\n| Beam search (num_beams) | ⚠️ 需改代码 | ~75 行 | 低（detect() 每步全文 re-parse，无增量 state 依赖） |\n\n**推荐回复 reviewer**：sampling 已支持（或极易添加），beam search 需约 75 行改动使 TypeConstraintProcessor 支持 per-beam state，架构上无阻塞。\n"
}
````

### Assistant

_source line 100 · 2026-05-16T19:20:45.117Z_

报告已写入 `artifacts/beam_sampling_feasibility.md`。

核心发现：
- **Sampling**（temperature/top_p）：**立即可行**，batch_size=1 下两层 processor 都正常工作，只需加 ~10 行 CLI 参数传递
- **Beam search**：**需改代码**（~75 行）。XGrammar 层天然支持多 beam（自动创建 per-beam GrammarMatcher），但 `TypeConstraintProcessor` 有单一共享 `self.state`，beam search 下多个 beam 会互相覆盖 state。修复方案是改为 `List[GenerationState]` per-beam 索引。由于 `PositionDetector.detect()` 每步对完整文本做 full re-parse（非增量），改动后无需处理 beam reorder，风险低。
